Parlay board

How the board is built These are the most confident moneylines left after Pixel’s Pick takes its legs, stacked in confidence order until the combined price reaches +100 or better. No game appears twice, so each ticket is genuinely different rather than a reshuffle of the same games. Short favourites are worth little on their own; combining them is what turns them into a payout worth the risk. It does not turn them into an edge — every leg still carries its own vig, and the tracker records how these actually land.

Full breakdown

Why this is a Pass

Central Michigan Chippewas is a Pass: the calibrated chance of winning is 30%. The price (+190) implies 34%, more than the model gives — the confidence is real but the book is charging for it.

Since 2023, college moneylines the model rated as a Pass have gone 112-144 (44%), returning -6.7% per unit staked — no play.

The model cannot separate these two: it still shows a side so the reasoning is visible, but this is not put forward as a pick.

Locked (). This is the pick of record: it will not change and it is what the tracker grades.

How the model sees it playing out

The power ratings see two closely matched teams, with only 1.9 points between them on raw strength. Overall, Central Michigan Chippewas is the 117th-rated team in the league (a real weakness) and Western Michigan Broncos is 106th (a real weakness). Central Michigan Chippewas then adds the home-field bump the model fits at 3.8 points. Rolling that together, the engine projects Central Michigan Chippewas by 2 and gives them a 55% chance to win outright.

Projected score: Central Michigan Chippewas 29, Western Michigan Broncos 27 against a market total of 56.

On how it plays out: Central Michigan Chippewas has the better ground matchup, a slight edge on the run. Expect a game that stays within one score into the fourth quarter, where late possessions carry outsized weight.

The market has this at Western Michigan Broncos -6. That is a real disagreement: the model wants 8.1 more points on Central Michigan Chippewas than the market is pricing, which is where the value on this game sits.

One caution: the projection sits near 3, a key number in football margins. Games cluster on it, so small errors in the estimate flip outcomes against the spread more often than the point difference suggests.

Both sides are starting their usual quarterbacks with no notable injury gap.
